CORPORATE PROFILE
公司概況
China Foods Limited (“China Foods” or the “Company” and together with its
subsidiaries, the “Group”), a subsidiary of COFCO Corporation (“COFCO”) and
the only focused beverage platform of COFCO, is listed on the main board of The
Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (Stock Code: 506).

The Company mainly manufactures, distributes, markets and sells the Coca-Cola
series products at 19 provincial-level administrative regions in the PRC through
COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited (“COFCO Coca-Cola”), a 65%-owned joint-
venture with The Coca-Cola Company. COFCO Coca-Cola offers twenty brands
of products in ten major types of beverages which include sparkling drink, juice,
water, milk drink, energy drink, tea, coffee, functional nutrition drink, sports
drink and plant-based protein drink.

中國食品有限公司（簡稱「中國食品」或「本公司」，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集
團」
 ）為中糧集團有限公司（「中糧」
               ）的附屬公司及中糧唯一的專業化飲料業務平
台，並於香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市（股份代號：506）。

本公司目前主要通過與可口可樂公司合作成立並持有65%權益的中糧可口可
樂飲料有限公司（簡稱「中糧可口可樂」）在國內19個省級行政區域從事生產、
配送、推廣和銷售可口可樂系列產品，向消費者提供包括汽水、果汁、水、乳
飲料、能量飲料、茶、咖啡、功能型營養素飲料、運動飲料及植物蛋白飲料等
10大品頖、20個品牌的產品。

                                On 26 April, Mr. Yu Xubo, the President of COFCO, resigned as the Chairman of the board of directors
                                of China Foods due to a change of work, and was replaced by Mr. Chen Lang, a Vice President of
                                COFCO.
                                於4月26日，中糧總裁于旭波先生因工作調動，辭任中國食品董事會主席職務，並由中糧副總裁陳朗

   A visit from the President of Coca-Cola (Latin America), its core
   management team and presidents of regional major bottling
   groups
   可口可樂拉丁美洲區總裁及其核心管理團隊和
   區域主要裝瓶集團總裁來訪學習

   Cola (Latin America), its core management team and
   presidents of major bottling groups in Latin America,
   made a special visit to communicate with the key
   management team of COFCO Coca-Cola. The visitors
   also visited the Beijing market, giving special attention
   to new product distribution and display execution under
   the new retail format, and gave high recognition for the
   overall performance of the Beijing market to COFCO
   Coca-Cola Beijing team.

   於5月14日，可口可樂拉丁美洲區總裁Alfredo Rivera先生及其核心管
   理團隊、拉丁美洲主要裝瓶集團總裁一行，專程來訪，與中糧可口可

   零售業態下市場新品鋪貨與陳列執行，對北京市場的整體表現給與了
   充分的肯定。

2019        SEPTEMBER 九月                                                                                             RISKS
Coca-Cola’s global hot drink project debuts at COFCO Coca-Cola
                                                                                         CONTAINMENT
可口可樂全球熱飲項目在中糧可口可樂首發上市
On 20 September, Mr. Chen Lang, the Chairman of the Company, Mr.
Manolo Arroyo, the President of Coca-Cola (Asia Pacific), and Ms. Luan
Xiuju, the then Managing Director of the Company, attended the debut
ceremony of Coca-Cola’s global hot drink project in Chengdu. The hot
drinks were specially developed for the China market by Coca-Cola at the
request of COFCO Coca-Cola to meet the drinking habits and needs of
China consumers. In 2019, COFCO Coca-Cola launched a total of 10 new




2019
products, which accounted for more than 8% of its sales revenue.

於9月20日，本公司主席陳朗先生、可口可樂亞太區總裁Manolo Arroyo先生、本公司時任董事總經理欒秀菊女士共同在成都
啓動可口可樂全球熱飲項目的首發儀式。熱飲產品是可口可樂應中糧可口可樂要求，為中國市場特別研發的新品，更好滿足
中國消費者的飲用習慣與需求。2019年，中糧可口可樂共上市10個系列新品，新品佔銷售收入比例超過8%。




                                 INTERNATIONAL
                                                  2019       NOVEMBER 十一月

                                                 Directors of China Foods visited COFCO Coca-Cola’s Huazhong Plant
                                                 中國食品董事調研中糧可口可樂華中廠
                                                 On 7 November, a board meeting of China Foods was held in Changsha. The
                                                 Chairman of the Company, Mr. Chen Lang, Non-executive Directors Ms. Xiao
                                                 Jianping and Mr. Qin Yelong, and Independent Non-executive Directors Mr. Mok
                                                 Wai Bun, Ben, Mr. Stephen Edward Clark and Mr. Li Hung Kwan, Alfred, had been
                                                 accompanied by the then Managing Director Ms. Luan Xiuju and an Executive
                                                 Director Mr. Shen Peng to visit COFCO Coca-Cola’s Huazhong Plant. During the
                                                 visit, the board of directors fully affirmed the plant’s success in accomplishing the
                                                 2019 annual performance targets in advance, and looked forward to the team’s
                                                 continue efforts in achieving better results.
                                                 於11月7日，中國食品董事會會議在長沙召開。本公司主席陳朗先生、非執
                                                 行董事肖建平女士、覃業龍先生，獨立非執行董事莫衛斌先生、祈
                                                 立德先生、李鴻鈞先生在時任董事總經理欒秀菊女士、執行
                                                 董事沈芃先生的陪同下對中糧可口可樂華中廠進行了
                                                 調研。調研中，董事會對華中廠提前完成2019全年
                                                 業績目標給予了充分肯定，期待華中廠團隊再接再
                                                 厲，再創佳績。

    2019


    DECEMBER 十二月
    Mr. Lu Jun, the Chairman of COFCO, visited Guiyang and assisted in the construction of a COFCO Coca-
    Cola Guizhou Plant
    中糧董事長呂軍先生親赴貴陽，協助中糧可口可樂籌建貴州工廠
    Guizhou is the only market in COFCO Coca-Cola’s franchise area whose sales volume have exceeded the break-even point
    without a local plant. We plan to complete the land contract and begin the construction of such in 2020, with an aim to
    starting production in 2021.

    貴州是中糧可口可樂經營區域內銷量超過盈虧平衡點尚未建廠的唯一市場。計劃在2020年完成土地簽約、啓動項目建
    設，爭取2021年投入生產。

    On 17 December, Mr. Lu Jun, the Chairman of COFCO, accompanied by Mr. Chen Lang, a Vice President of COFCO and
    the Chairman of China Foods, and Ms. Luan Xiuju, the then Managing Director of China Foods, visited Guiyang. During
    which Mr. Lu has discussed with Ms. Shen Yiqin, the Provincial Governor of Guizhou, and Mr. Zhao Deming, the Secretary
    of Guiyang Municipal Committee of the CPC, the matters related to the construction
    of a COFCO Coca-Cola Guizhou Plant and then paid a visit to a potential site for the
    plant on the following day.

    12月17日，中糧董事長呂軍先生親赴貴陽，拜會了貴州省長諶貽琴女士、貴陽
    市委書記趙德明先生就中糧可口可樂貴州工廠的籌建相關事宜進行討論。次日，
    呂軍董事長還親自考察了備選廠址。中糧副總裁兼中國食品董事長陳朗先生及中
    國食品時任董事總經理欒秀菊女士陪同會見和考察。



 BUSINESS
          OPTIMISATION
    2019        DECEMBER 十二月


    Mr. Qing Lijun appointed as the Managing
    Director of China Foods
    慶立軍先生接任中國食品董事總經理職務
    On 30 December, the Company announced that Ms. Luan Xiuju would
    retire from her positions as an Executive Director and the Managing
    Director of China Foods and be replaced by Mr. Qing Lijun, with effect
    from 1 January 2020.

    於12月30日，本公司宣佈自2020年1月1日起欒秀菊女士因退休辭任中
    國食品執行董事兼董事總經理職務，並由慶立軍先生接任。

        Dear Shareholders,                                                        致各位股東:

        2019: BUSINESS OPTIMISATION,                                              2019年：高質量，國際化，防
        GLOBALISATION AND RISK CONTAINMENT                                        風險！

        In 2019, China Foods’ work theme was “Business Optimisation,              2019年，中國食品的工作主題為「高質量、
        Globalisation and Risk Containment”. While maintaining steady             國際化、防風險」。在繼續保持穩健增長的
        growth, we focused on improving the quality of our operations,            同時，著力於經營質量的提升，同時對標國
        implemented benchmarking management to find points of                     際先進品牌消費品公司尋找差距、學習經
        weakness, and learned from the experiences of leading international       驗，完善自身管理制度和系統，防範運營風
        branded consumer goods companies to upgrade our management                險。
        system and minimize operational risks.

        In 2019, China Foods achieved good operating results, with steady         2019年，中國食品取得了良好的經營業
        growth in revenue and profits and new enhancements to operating           績。營收規模和利潤穩健增長，經營質量也
        quality. Through the introduction of new and high-margin packaging        有了新的提升。我們通過新品和高毛利包裝
        products, we improved the product structure and strengthened              產品的推動，提升產品結構質量，同時嚴格
        price management to achieve growth in revenue per unit case.              價格管理，實現了單標箱收入的提升；我們
        Through the development of ready-to-drink and new retail channels,        通過即飲渠道和新零售渠道的開發、優質客
        premium customer-oriented services and super city projects, we            戶的聚焦服務、超級城市項目的建設等，提

    INDUSTRIAL ENVIRONMENT                                                  行業環境

    According to statistics from the National Bureau of Statistics of       根據中國國家統計局統計數據，2019年中
    the PRC, China’s GDP achieved a year-on-year growth of 6.1%             國國內生產總值同比增長6.1%，全國居民
    and the per capita consumption expenditure recorded a year-on-          人均消費支出同比增長8.6%。隨著國家經
    year growth of 8.6% in 2019. With the steady development of the         濟穩健發展以及城鎮化程度的持續提升，
    national economy and the continuous improvement of the level of         2019年非酒精即飲行業總體收入同比保持
    urbanisation, the overall revenue of the non-alcoholic ready-to-drink   增長，本公司三大主要品類汽水、果汁和包
    industry maintained a year-on-year growth in 2019. The three main       裝水各自行業發展態勢不一，其中汽水及包
    product categories of the Company, namely sparkling drinks, juice       裝水行業收入較快，增長率高於非酒精即飲
    and packaged water, demonstrated different industry development         行業平均，而果汁行業收入則呈現負增長。
    trends, among which, sparkling drinks and packaged water had
    more rapid growth in revenue, which is higher than the average for
    the non-alcoholic ready-to-drink industry, and the juice category
    recorded negative growth in revenue.

    of high-margin products and the improvement in our packaging              善。

     LIQUIDITY AND FINANCIAL RESOURCES                                        流動資金及財務資源

     The Company’s treasury function operates as a centralised service        本公司資金部集中管理：
     for:

     INTRODUCTION                                                             緒論
     China Foods Limited (hereinafter referred to as the “Company” or         中國食品有限公司（以下簡稱「本公司」或「中
     “China Foods”, together with COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited           國食品」，連同持有65%股權的中糧可口可
     (hereinafter referred to as “COFCO Coca-Cola”), a 65%-owned              樂飲料有限公司（以下簡稱「中糧可口可樂」
     subsidiary, and its subsidiaries, collectively as the “Group”) follows   及其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）作為一家行業
     the social responsibility philosophy of “loyalty to national policies    內領先的飲料企業，傳承「忠於國計、良於
     and making people’s lives better”. In its corporate mission and          民生」的社會責任理念，致力於「奉獻時尚飲
     responsibility, it is “dedicated to modern beverages and creating        品，共創永恒價值」的企業使命和責任，努
     long-lasting value”, and it strives to achieve sustainable development   力實現企業自身和社會的可持續發展。
     for itself and for society.


     This report is the Company’s “Environmental, Social and Governance       本報告乃本公司的《環境、社會及管治報
     Report” (hereinafter referred to as the “Report”). Its contents          告》

     Governance Reporting Guide (the “ESG Guide”) in Appendix 27 of           稱「上市規則」）附錄二十七《環境、社會及

     of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”) and are in compliance         並遵守了相關規定。本報告範圍涵蓋本公
     with all relevant regulations. The scope of this Report covers the       司在中國的主要生產運營活動，報告期與
     major production and operational activities of the Company in            2019財政年度一致。本報告旨在向社會各
     China, and the reporting period is in line with the 2019 financial       界披露中國食品於2019年度在環境、社會
     year. The Report’s purpose is to summarise the environmental,            及管治(ESG)領域取得的工作成果，並應對
     social and governance (ESG) performance of China Foods in 2019,          各利益相關方之重點關切。
     and respond to the concerns of various stakeholders.


     The Company understands that the standard of its ESG                     本公司深刻理解我們在環境、社會及管治方
     management and performance is an important factor in achieving           面的管理水平和績效表現對實現本公司可持
     its strategic sustainable development objectives. We hope that the       續發展戰略目標的重要作用，我們希望通過
     Report will elicit constructive suggestions from our stakeholders to     本報告傾聽各利益相關方對本公司的良好建

     relevant ESG areas. Please send your feedback on the Report by           及管治相關領域的管理水平和績效表現。

        3.3 Occupational health and safety                                3.3 職業健康與安全
             The Company strives to create a safe and healthy               本公司努力為所有員工營造安
             working environment for all employees, and abides by all       全健康的工作環境。本公司積
             national and local occupational health and safety laws         極識別並遵循國家和地方層面有
             and regulations including the Labour Law of the People’        關職業健康和安全管理的法律法
             Republic of China（《中華人民共和國勞動法》）, the Law                       規，包括《中華人民共和國勞動

             of China on Work Safety（《中華人民共和國安全生產                           全管理方面，本集團制定了相關
             法》）. The Group has emplaced occupational health and            制度，包含安全生產與職業衛生
             safety management systems including Safety Production          管理規定、危險作業安全管理辦
             and Occupational Hygiene Management Regulations,               法、安全生產事故隱患排查治理
             Measures for Safety Management of Dangerous                    管理辦法等，同時制定了一系列
             Operations, and Measures for the Management of Safety          的安全操作流程規定。作為提升
             Production and Treatment for Accidents, as well as             本集團職業健康和安全管理水平
             operational safety procedures. The Company continues           的重要手段之一，本公司持續
             to expand and refine its occupational health and safety        推進各下屬公司╱工廠建立並
             management systems in its subsidiaries/plants in order         運行職業健康安全管理體系。
             to raise the Group’s standard. As of the end of 2019, 19       截至2019年底，本公司範圍內
             production units had passed OHSAS 18001 certification          19家生產單位全部通過OHSAS
             for occupational health and safety management                  18001職業健康安全管理體系的
             systems.                                                       認證。


             In terms of implementation of specific measures,               在落實有關制度規定的具體措施
             the Company requires its subordinate plants to                 方面：本公司要求下屬工廠皆需
             establish quality, environmental protection and safety         設立質量安全環保部，並設立安

             protection specialists responsible for plant safety            環境保護管理工作；各工廠內部
             and the environmental protection management.                   設立了EHS（環境、健康和安全）
             Furthermore, each plant formulated a compliance list on        法規遵循目錄清單，由專人負責
             EHS (environment, health and safety) regulations which         更新維護；工廠對危險性操作崗
             is renewed and maintained by the aforementioned                位和潛在危害因素進行識別，為
             specialists. The plants identify dangerous operational         操作員工配備勞動保護設備，如
             positions and potential hazards, provide their operators       護目鏡、安全帽、安全手套、安
             with personal protective equipment such as goggles,            全鞋等；對特殊作業實行嚴格的
             helmets, safety gloves and safety shoes, implement             審批和監管流程，並提供針對性

             such as fencing or lifelines where working at height is

        CONTAINMENT
GLOBALISATION                                                           CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT
                                                                                        企業管治報告



    China Foods Limited (the “Company” and together with its                 中國食品有限公司（「本公司」連同其附屬公
    subsidiaries, the “Group”) is committed to maintaining high              司統稱「本集團」）致力維持高水準的企業管
    standards of corporate governance. The board of directors of the         治標準。本公司董事會（「董事會」）相信良好
    Company (the “Board”) beliefs that good corporate governance             的企業管治水平及常規對本集團之效率及表
    standards and practices are crucial to improving the efficiency and      現以及維護股東、投資者、客戶及員工之利
    performance of the Group and to safeguarding the interests of our        益致為重要。
    shareholders, investors, customers and staff.

    CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                           企業管治常規

    The Company’s corporate governance practices are based on                本公司的企業管治常規乃根據香港聯合交易
    the principles and code provisions (the “Code Provisions”) of the        所有限公司證券上市規則（「上市規則」）附錄
    Corporate Governance Code (the “CG Code”) as set out in Appendix         十四《企業管治守則》（「企業管治守則」）所
    14 of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock         載的原則及守則條文（
                                                                                      「守則條文」）為基準。
    Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”).

    The Company has applied the principles of the CG Code and                於 截 至 2 0 1 9 年 1 2 月 3 1 日 止 年 度（「本 年

    Risk Management and Internal Control                                       風險管理及內部監控

    The Board acknowledges its responsibility for maintaining                  董事會確認其對維持全面及有效的風險管理
    comprehensive and effective risk management and internal control           和內部監控制度承擔責任，並通過董事會審
    systems, and for reviewing the effectiveness of these systems              核委員會檢討這些系統的有效性。
    through the audit committee of the Board.

    Sound risk management and internal control systems are based on            健全的風險管理和內部監控體系是基於本公
    the Company’s good business ethics and culture, the quality and            司良好的商業道德與文化、員工的素質與能
    capabilities of its employees, the direction of the Board, and also the    力、董事會的方向以及管理層的效率與能
    efficiency and capabilities of management.                                 力。

    Management of the Company, under the Board supervision, is                 本公司的管理層在董事會的監督下，負責風
    responsible for the design, implementation and monitoring of the           險管理和內部監控系統的設計，實施和監
    risk management and internal control systems.                              督。

    The Group has established a sound and comprehensive risk                   本集團已經建立起完善的全面風險管理和內
    management and internal control system, formed a systematic and            部監控體系，形成了科學有效的職責分工體
    effective system on segregation of duty and three lines of defense in      系，建立了風險管理三道防線，即業務部門
    risk management, with each of the business units as the first line of      為第一道防線；各級業務監督檢查部門為第
    defense; the business supervision and inspection departments at all        二道防線；內審部和監察部為第三道防線。
    levels as the second line of defense; and internal audit department
    and supervision department as the third line of defense.

    The Group has established its risk management and internal control         本集團依照COSO (Committee of Sponsoring
    systems based on the COSO (Committee of Sponsoring Organisation            Organisations of the Treadway Commisson)
    of the Treadway Commission) risk management framework.                     的風險管理框架搭建風險管理及內部監控系
                                                                               統。

    The Group’s risk management covers the Company’s headquarters              本集團的風險管理工作覆蓋本公司總部及各
    and its subsidiaries, covering various risks faced in operation and        下屬公司，內容涵蓋經營管理中面臨的各種
    management, and focusing on and continuously monitor the key               風險，並對其中的關鍵風險進行重點關注和
    risks.                                                                     持續監督。

    According to the risk management strategy, the Group conducts a            根據風險管理策略，結合本集團的戰略和經
    comprehensive risk assessment every two years in combination with          營目標，以及市場環境的變化，本集團每兩
    the Group’s strategic and business objectives and changes in the           年實施一次全面風險評估。識別對實現戰略
    market environment. Identify major risks that adversely affect the         目標造成不利影響的主要風險，並根據一套
    achievement of strategic objectives and prioritise the identified risks    標準準則評估及排列所識別風險的優先次
    based on a set of standard criteria and formulate a risk mitigation        序,制定風險緩解計劃以便將風險監控至可
    plan to monitor the risk to an acceptable level.                           接受水準。

     department has summarised and prioritized the evaluation results,        本集團2019年面對的重大風險。

     risks faced by the Group in 2019.

     and accountable persons, the management process and the                  及相應報告機制。

     relevant major risks have been effectively controlled. There has         2019年本集團未發生重大風險事項。
     been no material risk event occurred within the Group in 2019.

    Such systems and internal controls are designed to manage, rather         該等系統及內部監控的訂立旨在管理而非消
    than eliminate, the risk of failure to achieve business objectives.       除未能實現業務目標的風險,且只能作出合
    They can only provide reasonable, and not absolute, assurance             理而非絕對的保證可以防範重大事實陳述或
    against material misstatement or loss.                                    損失。

      1. CORPORATE INFORMATION                                                  1. 公司資料

          China Foods Limited (the “Company”) is a limited liability              中國食品有限公司（「本公司」）為一家

          China (the “PRC”).                                                      司。

      2. BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                   2. 編製基準

          The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                儘管本集團於報告期末有流動負債淨
          on a going concern basis notwithstanding that the Group had             額人民幣2,622,249,000元，本綜合

          reporting period. In preparing these consolidated financial             製此等綜合財務報表時，董事已審慎
          statements, the Directors have given careful consideration              考慮本集團目前及預期未來的流動資
          to the current and anticipated future liquidity of the Group.           金。經考慮（其中包括）(i)於報告期末
          Taking into account, inter alia, (i) the unutilised loan facilities     尚未使用的貸款授信；及(ii)本集團未
          at the end of the reporting period, and (ii) the expected net           來十二個月透過經營業務所產生的預
          cash inflows generated from the Group’s operations for the              期現金流入淨額，董事認為，本集團
          next twelve months. The Directors are of the opinion that the           將能夠應付其到期負債。因此，董事
          Group will be able to meet its liabilities as and when they fall        認為按持續經營基準編製此等綜合財
          due. Accordingly, the Directors consider that the preparation           務報表乃屬適當。

          basis is appropriate.

4. SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                        4. 主要會計政策

   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in                 本綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公
   accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. In addition,                   會所頒佈的香港財務報告準則而編
   the consolidated financial statements include applicable                    製。此外，本綜合財務報表亦按香港
   disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing of                  聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則
   Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited                      （「上市規則」）及按香港公司條例的要
   (“Listing Rules”) and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                 求作出適當披露。

   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on                 綜合財務報表按歷史成本基準編製，
   the historical cost basis except for certain properties that are            以公平值計量之若干投資物業則除
   measured at fair values at the end of each reporting period, as             外，於載列於下文之會計政策中解
   explained in the accounting policies set out below.                         釋。

   Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the                 歷史成本一般根據貨品及服務交易所
   consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                     付出之公平值計算。

   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset            公平值為市場於計量日在有序交易中
   or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between          出售資產所收取的價格或轉讓的負債
   market participants at the measurement date, regardless of                 所支付的價格，不論該價格是否可直
   whether that price is directly observable or estimated using               接觀察或以其他估值技巧估計。在估
   another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value                  計資產或負債之過程中，本集團已計
   of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the               算該資產或負債之特性，倘若市場參
   characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants           與人將考慮該特性而於計量日對該資
   would take those characteristics into account when pricing                 產或負債出價。於綜合財務報表作為
   the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for             計量及╱或披露公平值均按此基準而
   measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated               釐定，包含在香港財務報告準則第2號
   financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for             「以股代款」範圍內之以股份為基礎支
   share-based payment transactions that are within the scope                 付之交易，包含在香港財務報告準則
   of HKFRS 2 Share-based Payment, leasing transactions that                  第16號（自2019年1月1日起）或香港會
   are accounted for in accordance with HKFRS 16 (since 1                     計準則第17號（於應用香港財務報告準
   January 2019) or HKAS 17 (before application of HKFRS 16),                 則第16號前）範圍內之租賃交易，及若
   and measurements that have some similarities to fair value                 干類似公平值但並非公平值之計量，
   but are not fair value, such as net realisable value in HKAS 2             如香港會計準則第2號存貨可變現淨值
   Inventories or value in use in HKAS 36 Impairment of Assets.               或香港會計準則第36號資產之減值使
                                                                              用價值等則除外。

   A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into                非金融資產之公平值計量計及市場參
   account a market participant’s ability to generate economic                 與者通過使用其資產之最高及最佳用
   benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by               途或將其出售予將使用其最高及最佳
   selling it to another market participant that would use the asset           用途之另一市場參與者，而能夠產生
   in its highest and best use.                                                經濟利益之能力。

   For financial instruments and investment properties which                   就按公平值交易的金融工具及投資物
   are transacted at fair value and a valuation technique that                 業以及於其後期間使用不可觀察輸入
   unobservable inputs is to be used to measure fair value in                  數據計量公平值的估值技術而言，估
   subsequent periods, the valuation technique is calibrated so                值技術會進行調整，以致初步確認時
   that at initial recognition the results of the valuation technique          估值技術的結果等於交易價格。
   equals the transaction price.

          Basis of Consolidation                                                   合併基準
          The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial          本綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公
          statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                 司及其附屬公司所控制企業。當本公
          Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the               司符合以下要素時，則取得控制權：
          Company:

          •    has power over the investee;                                        •   可對投資對象行使權力；

          •    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its             •   因參與被投資對象而就浮動回報
               involvement with the investee; and                                      承擔風險或享有權利；及

          •    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.             •   有能力行使其權力以影響其回
                                                                                       報。

          The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if           倘有事實及情況顯示上文所述三個控
          facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one           制元素其中一項或超過一項之改變，
          or more of the three elements of control listed above.                   本集團重新評估是否已控制投資對
                                                                                   象。

          Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains              當本集團獲得一附屬公司之控制權開
          control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses              始綜合該附屬公司，而當本集團失去
          control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses             該附屬公司之控制權時結束綜合。尤
          of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are              其是年內所購入或出售附屬公司之收
          included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss from            入及支出由本集團獲得控制權當日起
          the date the Group gains control until the date when the Group           包括在綜合損益表直至本集團停止該
          ceases to control the subsidiary.                                        附屬公司之控制權日期為止。

   recognised is not allocated to any assets including goodwill,                作比較以進行減值測試。任何已確認
   that forms part of the carrying amount of the investment. Any                之減值虧損會成為投資賬面值之一部

          Revenue from Contracts with Customers                                  客戶合約收入
          The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                於（或就此）完成履約義務時，本集團
          obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or           確認收入，即於特定履約義務的相關
          services underlying the particular performance obligation is           商品或服務的「控制權」轉讓予客戶時
          transferred to the customer.                                           確認。

          A performance obligation represents a good or service (or              履約義務指不同的商品及服務（或一組
          a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of         商品或服務）或一系列大致相等的不同
          distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.            商品及服務。

          Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over        控制權隨時間轉移，而倘滿足以下其
          time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction        中一項標準，則收入乃參照完全滿足
          of the relevant performance obligation if one of the following         相關履約義務的進展情況而隨時間確
          criteria is met:                                                       認：

          •    the customer simultaneously receives and consumes                 •   於本集團履約時，客戶同時取得
               the benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the               並耗用本集團履約所提供的利
               Group performs;                                                       益；

          •    the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset              •   本集團之履約產生或強化一項資
               that the customer controls as the Group performs; or                  產，該資產於本集團履約時乃由
                                                                                     客戶控制；或

          •    the Group’s performance does not create an asset with             •   本集團的履約並未產生對本集團
               an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                  有替代用途的資產，且本集團對
               enforceable right to payment for performance completed                迄今已完成履約之付款具有可執
               to date.                                                              行之權利。

          Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the           否則，收入於客戶獲得商品或服務控
          customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.              制權的時間點確認。

          A contract asset represents the Group’s right to consideration in      合約資產指本集團就向客戶換取本集
          exchange for goods or services that the Group has transferred          團已轉讓的商品或服務收取代價的權
          to a customer that is not yet unconditional. It is assessed            利（尚未成為無條件）。其根據香港財
          for impairment in accordance with HKFRS 9. In contrast,                務報告準則第9號評估減值。相反，
          a receivable represents the Group’s unconditional right to             應收款項指本集團收取代價的無條件
          consideration, i.e. only the passage of time is required before        權利，即代價付款到期前僅需時間推
          payment of that consideration is due.                                  移。

          A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer     合約負債指本集團因已自客戶收取代
          goods or services to a customer for which the Group has                價（或已可自客戶收取代價），而須轉
          received consideration (or an amount of consideration is due)          讓商品或服務予客戶之義務。
          from the customer.

   Foreign Currencies                                                          外幣
   In preparing the financial statements of each individual group             在編製各個別集團實體的財務報表
   entity, transactions in currencies other than the functional               時，以該實體的功能貨幣以外貨幣
   currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are recognised                （外幣）結算的交易按交易當日的匯率
   at the rates of exchanges prevailing on the dates of the                   確認。於呈報期結束時，以外幣定值
   transactions. At the end of the reporting period, monetary items           的貨幣項目按該日的通行匯率重新換
   denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates            算。以公平值列賬並以外幣定值的非
   prevailing at that date. Non-monetary items carried at fair value          貨幣項目按釐定公平值當日的通行匯
   that are denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated                率重新換算。按歷史成本以外幣計量
   at the rates prevailing on the date when the fair value was                的非貨幣項目不予重新換算。
   determined. Non-monetary items that are measured in terms of
   historical cost in a foreign currency are not retranslated.

   Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary                  結算貨幣項目及重新換算貨幣項目所
   items, and on the retranslation of monetary items, are                      產生的匯兌差額於產生期間的損益確
   recognised in profit or loss in the period in which they arise.             認。

   Borrowing Costs                                                             借貸成本
   All borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the period          所有其他借貸成本於產生期間在損益
   in which they are incurred.                                                 確認。

   Government Grants                                                           政府補助
   Government grants are not recognised until there is reasonable              政府補助於可合理確認本集團將遵守
   assurance that the Group will comply with the conditions                    政府補助所附條件及將收取該補助時
   attaching to them and that the grants will be received.                     方會確認。

   Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on                       政府補助於本集團確認補助所擬定賠
   a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                      償的有關成本為支出的期間有系統地
   recognises as expenses the related costs for which the grants               於損益確認。具體而言，主要條件為
   are intended to compensate. Specifically, government grants                 本集團須購買、建設或以其他方式收
   whose primary condition is that the Group should purchase,                  購非流動資產的政府補助於綜合財務
   construct or otherwise acquire non-current assets are                       狀況表確認為遞延收入並按系統及合
   recognised as deferred income in the consolidated statement                 理的基準於有關資產之可使用年期轉
   of financial position and transferred to profit or loss on a                撥至損益。
   systematic and rational basis over the useful lives of the related
   assets.

          At the end of the reporting period, the Group reviews the             在報告期末，本集團覆核其使用壽命
          carrying amounts of its property, plant and equipment, right-         有限的物業、廠房及設備、使用權資

          determine whether there is any indication that those assets           存在任何跡象顯示這些資產已發生減
          have suffered an impairment loss. If any such indication exists,      值損失。如果存在此類跡象，則會對
          the recoverable amount of the relevant asset is estimated             資產的可收回金額作出估計，以確定
          in order to determine the extent of the impairment loss (if           減值損失的程度（如有）。具備無限可
          any). Intangible assets with indefinite useful lives are tested       使用年期之無形資產最少每年及當有
          for impairment at least annually, and whenever there is an            跡象顯示可能出現減值時進行減值測
          indication that they may be impaired.                                 試。

          The recoverable amount of property, plant and equipment,              單獨估計物業、廠房及設備、使用權
          right-of-use assets and intangible assets are estimated               資產和無形資產的可收回金額。如果
          individually, when it is not possible to estimate the recoverable     無法估計單個資產的可收回金額，本
          amount individually, the Group estimates the recoverable              集團會估計該資產所屬的現金產生單
          amount of the cash-generating unit to which the asset belongs.        位的可收回金額。

          In addition, the Group assesses whether there is indication that      此外，本集團對是否有跡象顯示公司
          corporate assets may be impaired. If such indication exists,          資產可能出現減值進行評估。倘存在
          corporate assets are also allocated to individual cash-generating     有關跡象，於可識別合理一致的分配
          units, when a reasonable and consistent basis of allocation can       基準時，公司資產亦會分配至個別現
          be identified, or otherwise they are allocated to the smallest        金產生單位，否則有關資產會分配至
          group of cash-generating units for which a reasonable and             可識別合理一致分配基準的現金產生
          consistent allocation basis can be identified.                        單位最小組別。

          Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs             可收回金額是指公平值減去銷售成本
          of disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the          後的餘額和使用價值兩者中的較高
          estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present           者。在評估使用價值時，預計未來現
          value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market      金流量會採用稅前折現率折現為現
          assessments of the time value of money and the risks specific         值，該稅前折現率反映對貨幣時間價
          to the asset (or a cash-generating unit) for which the estimates      值的當前市場評價及該資產（或現金產
          of future cash flows have not been adjusted.                          生單位）未針對該風險調整估計未來現
                                                                                金流量的特有的風險。

          If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a cash-generating           如果資產（或現金產生單位）的可收回
          unit) is estimated to be less than its carrying amount, the           金額估計低於其賬面金額，則將該資
          carrying amount of the asset (or a cash-generating unit) is           產（或現金產生單位）的賬面金額減至
          reduced to its recoverable amount. For corporate assets or            其可收回金額。當公司資產或部分公
          portion of corporate assets which cannot be allocated on a            司資產不能按合理及一致基準分配至
          reasonable and consistent basis to a cash-generating unit,            現金產生單位時，本集團會比較一組
          the Group compares the carrying amount of a group of cash-            現金產生單位的賬面金額（包括分配至
          generating units, including the carrying amounts of the               該組現金產生單位的公司資產或部分
          corporate assets or portion of corporate assets allocated to that     公司資產的賬面金額）與該組現金產生
          group of cash-generating units, with the recoverable amount of        單位的可收回金額。
          the group of cash-generating units.

   Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable                存貨是以存貨成本和可變現淨值兩者
   value. Costs of inventories are determined on a weighted                      中的較低者列賬。存貨成本採用加權

   completion and costs necessary to make the sale.                              銷售所需的估計成本後所得數額。

   Financial Instruments                                                         金融工具
   Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when a              金融資產和金融負債當實體成為金融
   group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of                 工具合同條款的訂約方時予以確定。
   the instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of financial               所有常規方式購買或出售的金融資產
   assets are recognised and derecognised on a trade date basis.                 應以交易日為基礎進行確認和終止確
   Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or sales of                      認。常規方式購買或出售是指按照法
   financial assets that require delivery of assets within the time              規或市場慣例所確立的時間安排來交
   frame established by regulation or convention in the market                   付資產的金融資產的購買或出售。
   place.

   Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured             除與客戶簽訂的合同產生的貿易應收
   at fair value except for trade receivables arising from contracts             款項按照香港財務報告準則第15條進
   with customers which are initially measured in accordance                     行計量外，金融資產和金融負債以公
   with HKFRS 15. Transaction costs that are directly attributable               平值進行初始計量。被歸入收購或發
   to the acquisition or issue of financial assets and financial                 行金融資產和金融負債的直接應佔的
   liabilities are added to or deducted from the fair value of the               交易費用於初始確認時計入金融資產
   financial assets or financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial         或金融負債的公平值，或從中扣除。
   recognition.

   The effective interest method is a method of calculating the                 實際利率法是計算金融資產或金融負
   amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and               債攤銷成本以及在相關期間內分配利
   of allocating interest income and interest expense over the                  息收支的一種方法。實際利率是指將
   relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate that                金融工具在預計存續期或更短期間內
   exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments                （如適當）的估計未來現金收款額（包括
   (including all fees and points paid or received that form an                 支付或收取的構成實際利率組成部分
   integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and          的所有費用和貼息、交易費用以及其
   other premiums or discounts) through the expected life of the                他溢價或折價）折現為該工具初始確認
   financial asset or financial liability, or, where appropriate, a             時賬面淨額所使用的利率。
   shorter period, to the net carrying amount on initial recognition.

   Definition of default                                                         違約定義
   For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an                   就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團認
   event of default occurs when information developed internally                 為，違約事件在內部制訂或得自外界
   or obtained from external sources indicates that the debtor is                來源的資料顯示債務人不大可能悉數
   unlikely to pay its creditors, including the Group, in full (without          向債權人（包括本集團）還款（未計及本
   taking into account any collaterals held by the Group).                       集團所持任何抵押品）時發生。

   Credit-impaired financial assets                                              信用減值金融資產
   A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events                  金融資產在一項或以上違約事件（對該
   that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash                   金融資產估計未來現金流量構成不利
   flows of that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a                  影響）發生時維持信用減值。金融資產
   financial asset is credit impaired includes observable data                   維持信用減值的證據包括有關下列事
   about the following events:                                                   件的可觀察數據：

  •     significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower;          •    發行人或借款人的重大財困；

  •     a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event;               •    違反合約（如違約或逾期事件）；

  •     the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual               •    借款人的貸款人因有關借款人財
        reasons relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty,                      困的經濟或合約理由而向借款
        having granted to the borrower a concession(s) that the                       人批出貸款人不會另行考慮的優
        lender(s) would not otherwise consider;                                       惠；

  •     it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter                     •    借款人將可能陷入破產或其他財
        bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation; or                              務重組；或

  •     the disappearance of an active market for that financial                 •    因財務困難導致金融資產無法在
        asset because of financial difficulties.                                      活躍市場繼續交易。

   Write-off policy                                                              撇銷政策
   The Group writes off a financial asset when there is information              資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財困及無實
   indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial difficulty            際收回可能時（例如對手方被清盤或已
   and there is no realistic prospect of recovery, for example,                  進入破產程序時），本集團則撇銷金融
   when the counterparty has been placed under liquidation or                    資產。經考慮法律意見後（倘合適），
   has entered into bankruptcy proceedings. Financial assets                     遭撇銷的金融資產可能仍須按本集團
   written off may still be subject to enforcement activities                    收回程序進行強制執行活動。撇銷構
   under the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into account                    成取消確認事項。任何其後收回在損
   legal advice where appropriate. A write-off constitutes a                     益中確認。
   derecognition event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised
   in profit or loss.

          Measurement and recognition of ECL                                     預期信用損失之計量及確認
          The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of             預期信用損失之計量為違約概率、違
          default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if         約虧損（即違約時虧損大小）及違約時
          there is a default) and the exposure at default. The assessment        風險敞口之函數。違約概率及違約虧
          of the probability of default and loss given default is based          損之評估乃基於歷史數據按前瞻性資
          on historical data adjusted by forward-looking information.            料作調整。預期信用損失的預估乃無
          Estimation of ECL effects on unbiased and probability-weighted         偏概率加權平均金額，以各自發生違
          amount that is determined with the respective risks of default         約的風險為權重確定。
          occurring as the weights.

          Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference between all          一般而言，預期信用損失為根據合約
          contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance         應付本集團之所有合約現金流量與本
          with the contract and the cash flows that the Group expects to         集團預期收取之所有現金流量之間的
          receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined at       差額（按初始確認時釐定之有效利率貼
          initial recognition.                                                   現）。

          Where ECL is measured on a collective basis or cater for cases         倘預期信用損失按集體基準計量或迎
          where evidence at the individual instrument level may not yet          合個別工具水平證據未必存在的情
          be available, the financial instruments are grouped on the             況，則金融工具按以下基準歸類：
          basis:

          •    Nature of financial instruments;                                  •   金融工具的性質；

          •    Ageing;                                                           •   賬齡；

          •    Nature, size and industry of debtors; and                         •   債務人的性質、規模及行業；及

          •    External credit ratings where available.                          •   外部信用評級（倘有）。

          The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to ensure             歸類工作經管理層定期檢討，以確保
          the constituents of each group continue to share similar credit        各組別成份繼續分擔類似信貸風險特
          risk characteristics.                                                  性。

          Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying              利息收入根據金融資產的賬面總額計
          amount of the financial asset unless the financial asset is credit     算，但金融資產發生信用減值的情況
          impaired, in which case interest income is calculated based on         除外，此時利息收入根據金融資產的
          amortised cost of the financial asset.                                 攤銷成本計算。

          The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in profit              本集團透過調整所有金融工具的賬面
          or loss for all financial instruments by adjusting their               值於損益中確認減值損益，惟應收賬
          carrying amount with exception of accounts receivables,                款、其他應收款項及應收關聯方款項
          other receivables and due from related parties where the               除外，相應調整透過虧損撥備賬確
          corresponding adjustment is recognised through a loss                  認。
          allowance account.

                                                                                才終止確認金融資產。

10. INCOME TAX EXPENSE (Continued)                                          10. 所得稅開支（續）

   Under the Law of the People’s Republic of China on Enterprise               根據中國企業所得稅法（「企業所得稅
   Income Tax (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation Regulation                    法」）及企業所得稅法實施條例，中國
   of the EIT Law, the tax rate of the PRC subsidiaries is 25% for             附屬公司適用中國企業所得稅率兩年
   both years.                                                                 均為25%。

   Taxes on profits assessable elsewhere have been calculated                  從其他地區賺取應課稅溢利則按照本

   Group operates.                                                             之現行稅率計算稅項。

      40. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                          40. 資本風險管理

          The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the        本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團的
          Group will be able to continue as a going concern while             實體能夠持續經營，同時透過優化債
          maximising the return to stakeholders through the optimisation      務及股權平衡最大限度地提高股東回
          of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy        報。自去年起本集團整體策略保持不
          remains unchanged from prior year.                                  變。

          The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which      本集團資本架構包括債務淨額，其中
          includes bank borrowings disclosed in note 29, net of bank          包括附註29所披露銀行借款（已扣減
          balances and cash and equity attributable to owners of the          銀行結餘及現金）及本公司擁有人應佔
          Company, comprising issued share capital, share premium and         權益（包括已發行股本、股份溢價及儲
          reserves.                                                           備）。

          its credit risks associated with its financial assets.                貸風險。
